  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "glas-gaibhnenn-c01",
      "source_id": "glas-gaibhnenn-s1",
      "quote": "in Irish folklore, is a prized fabulous cow of bounty (fertility) that yields profuse quantities of milk. The cow is owned variously by a smith who may be named Gaivnin (hence reinforcing the notion that the cow's name is eponymous after him) or by the hero Cian mac Cáinte",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Glas Gaibhnenn (Glas Ghaibhleann, Glas Gaivlen), sapi ajaib folklor Irlandia (Irish), susu berlimpah; milik pandai besi Gaivnin atau Cian mac Cáinte, setara Cian ayah Lugh.",
        "en": "Glas Gaibhnenn (Glas Ghaibhleann, Glas Gaivlen), a fabulous cow of Irish folklore with profuse milk; owned by the smith Gaivnin or Cian mac Cáinte, matching Cian father of Lugh."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "glas-gaibhnenn-c02",
      "source_id": "glas-gaibhnenn-s1",
      "quote": "The cow is stolen (or craftily regained) by Balar or Balor the strong-smiter. The hero, in order to fulfill the quest to recover the cow, is transported by a banshee to a tower where Balor's daughter is sequestered, to produce a child destined to kill Balor.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dicuri Balar atau Balor; pahlawan dibawa banshee ke menara putri Balor; anak yang ditakdirkan membunuh Balor.",
        "en": "Stolen by Balar or Balor; the hero taken by a banshee to Balor's daughter's tower; a child destined to kill Balor."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "glas-gaibhnenn-c03",
      "source_id": "glas-gaibhnenn-s2",
      "quote": "Scríobh Patricia Monaghan gur bhandia í Glas Ghaibhleann ceangailte go minic le haibhneacha.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Patricia Monaghan: Glas Ghaibhleann dewi yang sering dikaitkan dengan sungai; Patrick Power, Glas Gamhain.",
        "en": "Patricia Monaghan: Glas Ghaibhleann a goddess often linked with rivers; Patrick Power, Glas Gamhain."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "glas-gaibhnenn-c04",
      "source_id": "glas-gaibhnenn-s1",
      "quote": "Whereas the full name means \"Grey (cow) of the Smith\" according to Larminie, and \"Goibniu's Grey or Brindled (Cow)\" according to Rhys.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Larminie: Sapi Kelabu milik Pandai Besi; Rhys: Sapi Kelabu milik Goibniu; glas biru, hijau, kelabu.",
        "en": "Larminie: Grey cow of the Smith; Rhys: Goibniu's Grey cow; glas blue, green, grey."
      }
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Gwyllgi adalah anjing mitos dari Wales yang muncul sebagai hantu mastiff atau serigala hitam yang menakutkan dengan napas jahat dan mata merah menyala.",
    "en": "The gwyllgi is a mythical dog of Wales seen as a frightful phantom mastiff or black wolf with baleful breath and blazing red eyes.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "gwyllgi-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Gwyllgi (gabungan gwyllt \"liar\" atau gwyll \"senja\" dengan ci \"anjing\") adalah anjing mitos dari Wales yang tampil sebagai penampakan mengerikan berupa mastiff atau serigala hitam, bernapas jahat dan bermata merah menyala. Ia perwujudan Welsh dari sosok anjing hitam dalam folklor Inggris. Ia juga disebut Cwn Annwn (\"anjing dunia lain\") dan Cwn Cyrff (\"anjing mayat\"). Artikel it menyebutnya \"The Dog of Darkness\" dan menyatakan ia paling sering muncul di jalan sepi pada malam hari.",
      "en": "The gwyllgi (from gwyllt 'wild' or gwyll 'twilight' plus ci 'dog') is a mythical Welsh dog that takes the shape of a dreadful phantom mastiff or black wolf, its breath malign and its eyes burning red, the Welsh form of the black dog of English folklore. It is also called Cwn Annwn ('dogs of the otherworld') and Cwn Cyrff ('corpse dog'). The it article calls it 'The Dog of Darkness' and says it haunts lonely roads at night.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "gwyllgi-c01",
        "gwyllgi-c02",
        "gwyllgi-c03",
        "gwyllgi-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Banyak penampakannya dilaporkan di Wales timur laut, khususnya di celah Nant y Garth dekat Llandegla di Denbighshire, dan bahkan hingga Marchwiel di Wrexham. Dalam budaya populer, gwyllgi muncul antara lain dalam Blood of the Earth karya Faith Hunter.",
      "en": "Many sightings come from north-east Wales, especially the Nant y Garth pass near Llandegla in Denbighshire, and even as far off as Marchwiel in Wrexham. In popular culture the gwyllgi appears in, among others, Faith Hunter's Blood of the Earth.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "gwyllgi-c04",
        "gwyllgi-c06"
      ]
    }

    "id": "Peri houle adalah peri khas pesisir Selat Inggris di Haute-Bretagne dan Kepulauan Channel, yang tinggal di gua-gua pantai bernama houle.",
    "en": "Houles fairies are fairies of the Channel coast of Upper Brittany and the Channel Islands, dwelling in seaside caves called houles.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "houles-fairy-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Peri houle (Breton: Gwenn fadoù) adalah peri khas pesisir Selat Inggris, dari Cancale hingga Tréveneuc di Haute-Bretagne, sampai ke Kepulauan Channel, dan dikenal dari beberapa penggalan kisah di Cotentin. Mereka tinggal di gua dan ceruk pantai yang disebut houle. Dikenal sangat indah, abadi dan sangat kuat, mereka peka terhadap garam. Mereka cenderung baik hati, hidup berkelompok, mencuci sendiri, memanggang roti atau menggembalakan ternak, menikah dengan peri lelaki, dan dilayani goblin prajurit bernama Fions.",
      "en": "Houles fairies (Breton Gwenn fadoù) belong to the Channel coast from Cancale to Tréveneuc in Upper Brittany and on to the Channel Islands, with a few story fragments from the Cotentin. They dwell in seaside caves and hollows called houles. Said to be splendid, immortal and very powerful, they are vulnerable to salt. Mostly kindly, they live in communities, do their washing, bake bread or herd flocks, take male fairies as husbands, and have warrior goblins, the Fions, as servants.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "houles-fairy-c01",
        "houles-fairy-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Mereka membantu manusia dengan berbagai cara, memberi makanan dan benda ajaib, tetapi marah bila tidak dihormati atau bila seseorang mampu melihat penyamaran mereka tanpa izin. Kumpulan Paul Sébillot dalam bahasa Prancis dan Gallo memuat sekitar lima puluh kisah dan penggalan legenda tentang mereka; orang Haute-Bretagne menganggap mereka \"setengah dewa\" dan mungkin memujanya. Kepercayaan ini sangat merosot pada abad ke-19 di bawah pengaruh Kristen dan para guru.",
      "en": "They help people in many ways, giving food and enchanted objects, but grow angry when slighted or when someone gains the power to see through their disguises without consent. Paul Sébillot's collections in French and Gallo hold about fifty tales and legend fragments about them; the Upper Bretons regarded them as 'semi-divinities' and probably worshipped them locally. Belief faded sharply in the 19th century under the influence of Christianity and schoolteachers.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "houles-fairy-c03",
        "houles-fairy-c04",
        "houles-fairy-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "houles-fairy-c01",
      "source_id": "houles-fairy-s1",
      "quote": "are fairies specific to the Channel coast, stretching from Cancale to Tréveneuc in Upper Brittany, to the Channel Islands, and known from a few fragments of stories in the Cotentin region. They live in coastal caves and caverns known as houles. Reputed to be magnificent, immortal and very powerful, they are sensitive to salt.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Peri houle (Gwenn fadoù), pesisir Selat Inggris (Channel) dari Cancale ke Tréveneuc, Haute-Bretagne (Upper Brittany), Kepulauan Channel (Channel Islands), Cotentin; gua pantai houle; abadi, kuat, peka garam; Breton.",
        "en": "Houles fairies (Gwenn fadoù), Channel coast from Cancale to Tréveneuc, Upper Brittany, the Channel Islands, Cotentin; coastal caves called houles; immortal, powerful, salt-sensitive; Breton."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "houles-fairy-c02",
      "source_id": "houles-fairy-s1",
      "quote": "Rather benevolent, the swell fairies described in local stories live in communities, do their own laundry, bake their own bread or tend their own flocks, marry male fairies and are served by warrior goblins called Fions.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Baik hati, hidup berkelompok, mencuci, memanggang roti, menggembala, dilayani goblin prajurit Fions.",
        "en": "Benevolent, communal, launder, bake bread, tend flocks, served by warrior goblins called Fions."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "houles-fairy-c03",
      "source_id": "houles-fairy-s1",
      "quote": "They come to the aid of humans in many ways, providing food and enchanted objects, but get angry if anyone disrespects them or acquires the power to see their disguises without their consent.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Membantu manusia dengan makanan dan benda ajaib; marah bila tak dihormati.",
        "en": "Help humans with food and enchanted objects; angry when disrespected."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "houles-fairy-c04",
      "source_id": "houles-fairy-s1",
      "quote": "Paul Sébillot's collections, in French and Gallo, have yielded some fifty tales and fragments of legends evoking these creatures. The houles fairies, considered \"semi-divinities\", were probably worshipped locally by the Upper Bretons.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Paul Sébillot, bahasa Prancis (French) dan Gallo, sekitar lima puluh kisah; setengah dewa dipuja orang Haute-Bretagne (Upper Bretons).",
        "en": "Paul Sébillot, French and Gallo, some fifty tales; semi-divinities worshipped by the Upper Bretons."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "houles-fairy-c05",
      "source_id": "houles-fairy-s1",
      "quote": "Belief in them waned considerably in the 19th century, under the religious influence of Christianity and teachers.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kepercayaan merosot pada abad ke-19 karena Kristen (Christianity) dan guru.",
        "en": "Belief waned in the 19th century due to Christianity and teachers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "houles-fairy-c06",
      "source_id": "houles-fairy-s2",
      "quote": "Grottenfeen (französisch fées des houles, englisch houles fairies) sind mythische Wesen aus der bretonischen Folklore, die entlang der Kanalküste, von Cancale bis Tréveneuc in der Haute-Bretagne sowie auf den Kanalinseln wie Guernsey, beheimatet sind.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel de: Grottenfeen (fées des houles), folklor Breton, Cancale hingga Tréveneuc, Haute-Bretagne, Guernsey; Prancis.",
        "en": "de article: Grottenfeen (fées des houles), Breton folklore, Cancale to Tréveneuc, Haute-Bretagne, Guernsey; France."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "houles-fairy-c07",
      "source_id": "houles-fairy-s2",
      "quote": "Sie werden als schöne, mächtige und fast unsterbliche Wesen beschrieben, die jedoch durch den Kontakt mit Salz sterblich werden können.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Indah, kuat, hampir abadi, menjadi fana karena kontak dengan garam.",
        "en": "Beautiful, powerful, nearly immortal, made mortal by contact with salt."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Jimmy Squarefoot adalah makhluk legenda berkaki dua berkepala babi dalam folklor Manx di Pulau Man.",
    "en": "Jimmy Squarefoot is a legendary two-legged, pig-headed creature of Manx folklore on the Isle of Man.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "jimmy-squarefoot-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam folklor Manx, Jimmy Squarefoot adalah makhluk legenda berkaki dua dan berkepala babi yang hidup di Pulau Man. Ia biasanya digambarkan sebagai lelaki berkepala babi dengan taring besar, atau sebagai babi hutan putih besar. Konon ia dulu ditunggangi seorang Foawr, raksasa pelempar batu yang tinggal di Cronk yn Irree Lhaa dan mungkin suami Caillagh ny Groamagh; setelah pasangan itu bertengkar dan lenyap, babi peliharaan mereka menjadi Jimmy Squarefoot. Artikel de menyebut kakinya yang besar dibalut kain belacu sehingga tampak persegi, asal nama \"Squarefoot\".",
      "en": "In Manx folklore Jimmy Squarefoot is a legendary pig-headed creature walking on two legs on the Isle of Man. He is usually pictured as a man with a pig's head and great tusks, or as a big white boar. He is said once to have been ridden by a Foawr, a stone-hurling giant of Cronk yn Irree Lhaa who was perhaps husband to the Caillagh ny Groamagh; when the couple quarrelled and vanished, their pet boar became Jimmy Squarefoot. The de article explains that his large feet, wrapped in calico, look square, hence the name.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "jimmy-squarefoot-c01",
        "jimmy-squarefoot-c02",
        "jimmy-squarefoot-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia dikatakan tinggal di perbukitan Arbory dan Malew, terutama dekat jembatan Sungai Grenaby, dan punya gua dekat South Barrule tempat ia membawa manusia ke dunia bawah. Ia mungkin terkait dengan kisah buggane di Rushen yang dapat berubah dari manusia menjadi babi hitam. Pada akhir abad ke-19 dan awal abad ke-20, para pelaut memakai nama Jimmy Squarefoot untuk menyebut iblis.",
      "en": "His home is placed in the Arbory and Malew hills, chiefly by the Grenaby river bridge, and to have a cave by South Barrule through which he takes people to the underworld. He may be linked to the tale of a buggane in Rushen that could switch between man and black pig. Around the late 1800s and early 1900s sailors used his name for the devil.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "jimmy-squarefoot-c03",
        "jimmy-squarefoot-c04"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Kilmoulis adalah versi buruk rupa dari brownie dalam folklor perbatasan Inggris-Skotlandia yang menghuni penggilingan.",
    "en": "The kilmoulis is an ugly kind of brownie from Anglo-Scottish border folklore that haunts mills.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "kilmoulis-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam folklor perbatasan Inggris dan Skotlandia (Anglo-Scottish border), kilmoulis adalah versi buruk rupa dari brownie yang konon menghuni penggilingan. Hidungnya sangat besar dan ia tidak punya mulut, sehingga ia menghirup makanannya lewat hidung. Ia bekerja keras untuk tukang giling, tetapi juga gemar mengerjai dan berbuat iseng; bantuannya biasanya cukup untuk mengimbangi makanan yang ia habiskan dan kekacauan yang ia buat.",
      "en": "In the folklore of the Anglo-Scottish border the kilmoulis is an ugly variety of brownie said to haunt mills. With a huge nose and no mouth at all, he must breathe his food in through his nose. He labours hard for the miller but loves tricks and mischief, though his help generally makes up for what he eats and the trouble he causes.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "kilmoulis-c01",
        "kilmoulis-c02",
        "kilmoulis-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam budaya populer, kilmoulis dipakai sebagai makhluk fey dalam permainan Dungeons & Dragons, muncul dalam permainan video Folklore, dan sebagai monster dalam buku Witcher karya Andrzej Sapkowski.",
      "en": "In popular culture the kilmoulis became a fey creature in Dungeons & Dragons, appears in the video game Folklore, and is a monster in Andrzej Sapkowski's Witcher books.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "kilmoulis-c03"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Krasnoludek atau krasnal adalah nama Polandia untuk gnome atau kurcaci mitologis dalam banyak dongeng, bertubuh kecil dan bertopi merah runcing.",
    "en": "Krasnoludek, or krasnal, is the Polish name for a mythical gnome or dwarf of many folk tales, small and wearing a pointed red hat.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "krasnoludek-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Krasnoludek atau krasnal adalah nama Polandia untuk jenis gnome atau kurcaci mitologis yang umum dalam dongeng Polandia dan terjemahan. Mereka mirip manusia kecil dan memakai topi merah runcing. Kurcaci mitologis ini berasal dari tradisi Jermanik dan muncul dalam dongeng Polandia pada abad ke-16 atau ke-17. Kata krasnoludek berasal dari bahasa Polandia kuno krasny (\"merah, berwarna\" atau \"elok\") dan ludek (orang kecil).",
      "en": "Krasnoludek or krasnal is the Polish name for a mythical kind of gnome or dwarf found in many Polish and translated folk tales. They look like little people and wear pointed red hats. The mythical dwarf is Germanic in origin and entered Polish folk tales in the 16th or 17th century. The word combines Old Polish krasny, 'red, colourful' or 'good-looking', with ludek, 'little person'.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "krasnoludek-c01",
        "krasnoludek-c02",
        "krasnoludek-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut artikel ru, dalam mitologi Polandia dan Belarus krasnoludek adalah bangsa kerdil yang dianggap pelindung rumah manusia; kepercayaan itu mungkin berasal dari keyakinan akan jiwa leluhur. Di kota Wrocław, patung perunggu untuk Orange Alternative, gerakan anti-komunis yang maskotnya krasnoludek, mengilhami ratusan patung kurcaci lain yang kini menjadi daya tarik wisata.",
      "en": "The ru article says that in Polish and Belarusian mythology the krasnoludki are a dwarf folk seen as guardians of human homes, a belief perhaps rooted in ideas about ancestors' souls. In Wrocław a bronze statue honouring the Orange Alternative, an anti-communist movement whose mascot is a krasnoludek, inspired hundreds of further dwarf figures that now draw tourists.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "krasnoludek-c04",
        "krasnoludek-c05"
      ]
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Morvarc'h (\"kuda laut\" dalam bahasa Breton) adalah kuda ajaib legenda Bretagne yang dapat berlari di atas ombak dan menyemburkan api dari lubang hidungnya.",
    "en": "Morvarc'h ('sea horse' in Breton) is a fabulous horse of Breton legend that gallops over the waves and breathes fire from its nostrils.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "morvarc-h-c01",
      "morvarc-h-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Morvarc'h, yang dalam bahasa Breton berarti \"kuda laut\", adalah kuda ajaib legenda Bretagne yang muncul dalam dua dongeng yang digubah ulang pada abad ke-19 dan ke-20. Meski namanya ada dalam sumber lebih tua, kuda ini diciptakan atau ditafsirkan ulang oleh Charles Guyot, yang menamainya Morvark dalam versinya tentang legenda kota Ys pada 1926. Kuda ini milik Malgven, \"Ratu Utara\", yang memberikannya kepada suaminya Raja Gradlon. Ia mampu berlari di atas ombak, berbulu hitam, dan menyemburkan api dari lubang hidungnya.",
      "en": "Morvarc'h, Breton for 'sea horse', is a wondrous horse of Breton legend found in two folktales retold in the 19th and 20th centuries. Its name occurs in older sources, but the horse itself was invented or reimagined by Charles Guyot, who called it Morvark in his 1926 version of the legend of the city of Ys. It belongs to Malgven, the 'Queen of the North', who gives it to her husband King Gradlon. It can gallop across the waves, has a black coat, and breathes flames from its nostrils.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "morvarc-h-c01",
        "morvarc-h-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia juga muncul dalam dongeng Breton tentang Raja Marc'h dari Cornouaille: dalam perburuan rusa ia tewas oleh panah penunggangnya sendiri, yang dibelokkan sihir Dahud, putri Malgven, lalu Dahud memasang telinga kuda itu di kepala Raja Marc'h. Legenda ini menjadi subjek patung berkuda di Argol dan di Katedral Saint-Corentin di Quimper. Namanya tercatat dalam kamus Grégoire de Rostrenen tahun 1732.",
      "en": "It also appears in a Breton tale of King Marc'h of Cornouaille: during a stag hunt it is killed by its own rider's arrow, turned back by a spell of Dahud, Malgven's daughter, who then sets the horse's ears on King Marc'h's head. The legend inspired equestrian statues at Argol and on Saint Corentin's Cathedral in Quimper. The name is recorded in Grégoire de Rostrenen's dictionary of 1732.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "morvarc-h-c03",
        "morvarc-h-c04",
        "morvarc-h-c05",
        "morvarc-h-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "morvarc-h-c01",
      "source_id": "morvarc-h-s1",
      "quote": "is the name of a fabulous horse of Breton legend found in two folktales reworked in the 19th and 20th centuries. Though its name appears in older sources, it was invented or reinterpreted by Charles Guyot, who named it Morvark in his version of the legend of the city of Ys in 1926. It belongs to the \"Queen of the North\" Malgven, who gives it to her husband King Gradlon.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Morvarc'h (kuda laut, Breton), legenda Bretagne (Breton, Prancis); Charles Guyot, Morvark, kota Ys, 1926; milik Malgven, Ratu Utara (Queen of the North), untuk Raja (King) Gradlon. Dua dongeng digubah ulang abad ke-19 dan ke-20 (19th, 20th).",
        "en": "Morvarc'h (sea horse, Breton), of Breton legend; Charles Guyot, Morvark, city of Ys, 1926; Malgven's, Queen of the North, given to King Gradlon. Two folktales reworked in the 19th and 20th centuries."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morvarc-h-c02",
      "source_id": "morvarc-h-s1",
      "quote": "Endowed with the ability to gallop on the waves, Morvarc'h is described as having a black coat and as breathing flames through its nostrils.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Berlari di atas ombak, bulu hitam, menyemburkan api.",
        "en": "Gallops on the waves, black coat, breathes flames."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morvarc-h-c03",
      "source_id": "morvarc-h-s1",
      "quote": "In the course of a deer hunt it is killed by its own rider's arrow, which has been turned around by the spell of Dahud, the daughter of Malgven. She then puts the ears of the horse Morvarc'h on the head of King Marc'h, who seeks in vain to hide them.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tewas oleh panah yang dibelokkan sihir Dahud, putri Malgven; telinganya di kepala Raja (King) Marc'h dari Cornouaille.",
        "en": "Killed by an arrow turned by Dahud's spell, Malgven's daughter; its ears on King Marc'h of Cornouaille."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morvarc-h-c04",
      "source_id": "morvarc-h-s1",
      "quote": "it is the subject of equestrian statues in the town of Argol and in Saint Corentin's Cathedral in Quimper.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Patung berkuda di Argol dan Katedral Saint-Corentin (Saint Corentin's Cathedral), Quimper.",
        "en": "Equestrian statues at Argol and Saint Corentin's Cathedral, Quimper."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morvarc-h-c05",
      "source_id": "morvarc-h-s1",
      "quote": "The name Morvarc'h means \"sea horse\" or \"marine horse\" in Breton. It appears in Grégoire de Rostrenen's [fr] dictionary, published in 1732.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Morvarc'h berarti kuda laut; kamus Grégoire de Rostrenen, 1732.",
        "en": "Morvarc'h means sea horse; Grégoire de Rostrenen's dictionary, 1732."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "morvarc-h-c06",
      "source_id": "morvarc-h-s2",
      "quote": "Dotado de la facultad de galopar sobre las olas, Morvarc'h está descrito como cubierto de sombras y expira llamas por las fosas nasales.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel es: berlari di atas ombak dan menyemburkan api dari hidung.",
        "en": "es article: gallops on the waves and breathes flames from its nostrils."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Nimerigar adalah ras orang kecil legenda dalam folklor suku Shoshone di Pegunungan Rocky, Amerika Utara, yang menembakkan panah beracun dari busur mungil.",
    "en": "The Nimerigar are a legendary race of little people in the folklore of the Shoshone of North America's Rocky Mountains, shooting poisoned arrows from tiny bows.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "nimerigar-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Nimerigar adalah ras orang kecil legenda dalam folklor suku Shoshone di Pegunungan Rocky, Amerika Utara. Menurut kisah Shoshone, mereka bangsa yang agresif dan menembakkan panah beracun dari busur mungil. Nama Nimerigar kurang lebih berarti \"pemakan manusia\" dalam bahasa Shoshone dan Paiute. Mereka diyakini membunuh anggota kaumnya sendiri dengan pukulan di kepala bila terlalu sakit untuk tetap berperan dalam masyarakat. Artikel de menyebut mereka konon dulu mendiami wilayah Wyoming.",
      "en": "Shoshone lore from the Rocky Mountains of North America tells of the Nimerigar, a legendary race of little people. Shoshone stories call them aggressive, loosing poisoned arrows from tiny bows. Nimerigar means roughly 'people eaters' in the Shoshone and Paiute tongues. They were thought to kill their own sick with a blow to the head once they could no longer take part in the group's life. The de article says they once lived in what is now Wyoming.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "nimerigar-c01",
        "nimerigar-c02",
        "nimerigar-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Pada 1932 ditemukan mumi San Pedro Mountains setinggi 14 inci di sebuah gua 60 mil selatan Casper, Wyoming, yang memunculkan pertanyaan tentang kebenaran kisah Nimerigar. Namun Universitas Wyoming menyimpulkan itu jasad bayi anencephalic yang kelainan tengkoraknya membuatnya tampak seperti orang dewasa mini; mumi kedua yang diperiksa George Gill pada 1990-an juga bayi anencephalic, dan uji DNA menunjukkan asal penduduk asli Amerika.",
      "en": "In 1932 the San Pedro Mountains mummy, 14 inches tall, was found in a cave 60 miles south of Casper, Wyoming, raising questions about the Nimerigar tales. The University of Wyoming, however, concluded it was an anencephalic infant whose skull deformity made it look like a tiny adult; a second mummy studied by George Gill in the 1990s was likewise an anencephalic infant, and DNA showed Native American ancestry.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "nimerigar-c03",
        "nimerigar-c04"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Nuggle adalah kuda air mitos dalam folklor Shetland, makhluk nokturnal jantan yang lebih suka iseng daripada mencelakai.",
    "en": "The nuggle is a mythical water horse of Shetland folklore, a nocturnal male creature given to pranks rather than malice.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "nuggle-c01",
      "nuggle-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Nuggle, njuggle atau neugle adalah kuda air mitos terutama dari folklor Shetland, di beberapa bagian kepulauan disebut juga shoepultie atau shoopiltee. Makhluk nokturnal yang selalu jantan ini sesekali dikaitkan dengan Kepulauan Orkney, tetapi lebih sering dengan sungai, aliran air dan loch di Shetland. Ia mudah dikenali dari ekornya yang mirip roda dan, tidak seperti each-uisge atau nuckelavee yang jahat, wataknya cukup lembut, lebih gemar iseng dan membuat kekacauan kecil.",
      "en": "The nuggle, njuggle or neugle is a mythical water horse mostly of Shetland folklore, called shoepultie or shoopiltee in some parts of the islands. Always male and active by night, it is now and then linked with Orkney but more often with the rivers, burns and lochs of Shetland. Its wheel-like tail gives it away, and unlike the wicked each-uisge or nuckelavee it is fairly mild, preferring tricks and mischief to real harm.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "nuggle-c01",
        "nuggle-c02",
        "nuggle-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Nuggle juga ditemukan di dekat kincir air dan tak pernah jauh dari air. Ia dapat menyamar dalam banyak wujud tetapi biasanya memilih wujud kuda yang menarik, dan tak pernah menjadi manusia. Bila berhasil memperdaya orang untuk menungganginya, dengan berpura-pura jinak di tepi jalan, ia langsung lari ke air terdalam. Jessie Saxby menduga ketakutan akan nuggle menjauhkan anak-anak dari air dalam dan kincir air.",
      "en": "Nuggles also lurked by watermills and never went far from water. Though able to take many shapes, it usually chose that of a fine horse and never became a man. If it fooled a passer-by into mounting it, by standing quietly and tamely beside a path, it at once made for the nearest deep water. Jessie Saxby suggested that fear of the nuggle kept children away from deep water and mills.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "nuggle-c03",
        "nuggle-c05",
        "nuggle-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "nuggle-c01",
      "source_id": "nuggle-s1",
      "quote": "is a mythical water horse of primarily Shetland folklore where it is also referred to as a shoepultie or shoopiltee on some parts of the islands. A nocturnal creature that is always of a male gender",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nuggle (njuggle, neugle), kuda air folklor Shetland, shoepultie atau shoopiltee; nokturnal, selalu jantan; Kepulauan Orkney (Orkney), loch.",
        "en": "Nuggle (njuggle, neugle), water horse of Shetland folklore, shoepultie or shoopiltee; nocturnal, always male; Orkney, lochs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nuggle-c02",
      "source_id": "nuggle-s1",
      "quote": "He is easily recognised by his distinctive wheel-like tail and, unlike his evil counterparts the each-uisge or the nuckelavee, has a fairly gentle disposition being more prone to playing pranks and making mischief rather than having malicious intents.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ekor seperti roda; tak seperti each-uisge atau nuckelavee, wataknya lembut dan suka iseng.",
        "en": "Wheel-like tail; unlike the each-uisge or nuckelavee, gentle and prankish."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nuggle-c03",
      "source_id": "nuggle-s1",
      "quote": "The entity was capable of assuming many disguises but generally favoured the form of an attractive horse; he never assumed a human form.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menyamar dalam banyak wujud, biasanya kuda menarik, tak pernah manusia; dekat kincir air.",
        "en": "Assumes many disguises, usually an attractive horse, never human; near watermills."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nuggle-c04",
      "source_id": "nuggle-s2",
      "quote": "El nuggle, njuggle o neugle es un caballo de agua mítico del folklore de las Islas Shetland, una criatura nocturna que siempre es macho.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel es: kuda air mitos Shetland, nokturnal, selalu jantan.",
        "en": "es article: mythical water horse of Shetland, nocturnal, always male."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nuggle-c05",
      "source_id": "nuggle-s1",
      "quote": "If the nuggle had successfully tricked an unsuspecting passer-by into mounting him, usually by pretending to be tame and standing tranquilly at the side of a path, he would immediately make for the nearest deep water.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Memperdaya orang menungganginya lalu lari ke air terdalam.",
        "en": "Tricks a passer-by into mounting, then heads for deep water."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nuggle-c06",
      "source_id": "nuggle-s1",
      "quote": "Jessie Saxby suggests fear of the nuggle prevented children venturing too close to deep water or watermills",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jessie Saxby: ketakutan akan nuggle menjauhkan anak dari air dalam dan kincir air.",
        "en": "Jessie Saxby: fear of the nuggle kept children from deep water and watermills."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Ratu Elphame adalah ratu peri dalam kepercayaan rakyat Dataran Rendah Skotlandia dan Inggris Utara, yang disebut dalam pengadilan penyihir Skotlandia.",
    "en": "The Queen of Elphame is the elfin queen of Faerie in the folk belief of Lowland Scotland and Northern England, named in Scottish witch trials.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "queen-of-elphame-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam kepercayaan rakyat Dataran Rendah Skotlandia dan Inggris Utara, Ratu Elphame adalah ratu peri dari dunia peri, yang disebut dalam pengadilan penyihir Skotlandia. Dalam balada dan teks sezaman ia juga disebut Queen of Elphane, Elphen, atau Ratu Peri, setara dengan Ratu Peri yang memerintah Faërie. Tokoh dalam pengadilan penyihir ini punya banyak kesamaan dengan legenda Thomas the Rhymer dan Tam Lin. Ejaan dalam transkrip pengadilan adalah \"Quene of Elfame\"; makna \"Elf-home\" hanyalah dugaan editor modern Robert Pitcairn.",
      "en": "In the folk belief of Lowland Scotland and Northern England the Queen of Elphame is the elfin queen of Faerie, mentioned in Scottish witch trials. Ballads and texts of the time also call her Queen of Elphane, Elphen or of the Fairies, the equivalent of the Queen who rules Faërie. As she appears in the trials she has much in common with the legends of Thomas the Rhymer and Tam Lin. The trial records spell it 'Quene of Elfame'; the sense 'Elf-home' is only a guess by the modern editor Robert Pitcairn.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "queen-of-elphame-c01",
        "queen-of-elphame-c02",
        "queen-of-elphame-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam pengadilan Alison Pearson tahun 1588, terdakwa mengaku berkali-kali dibawa ke Elfame dan mengenal sang Ratu; para peri bawahannya, termasuk William Simpson, mengajarinya tanaman obat dan seni penyembuhan. Artikel fi mencatat Pearson dibakar sebagai penyihir, dan Andro Man yang diadili pada 1598 menyebut sang ratu \"Quene of Elphen\" dan istri iblis.",
      "en": "At the 1588 trial of Alison Pearson the accused said she had often been carried to Elfame and knew the Queen; her elfin followers, among them William Simpson, taught her herbs and the art of healing. The fi article notes that Pearson was burned as a witch, and that Andro Man, tried in 1598, called the queen 'Quene of Elphen' and the devil's wife.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "queen-of-elphame-c04",
        "queen-of-elphame-c05"
      ]
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Rhita Gawr adalah raksasa dalam cerita rakyat Welsh yang memakai mantel dari janggut raja-raja dan dibunuh Raja Arthur di Yr Wyddfa.",
    "en": "Rhita Gawr is a giant of Welsh folk tales who wore a cloak of kings' beards and was killed by King Arthur on Yr Wyddfa.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "rhita-gawr-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Rhita Gawr adalah tokoh cerita rakyat Welsh, raksasa yang konon memiliki mantel yang dibuat dari janggut raja-raja. Ia menuntut janggut Raja Arthur; Arthur menolak, dan keduanya bertarung di Yr Wyddfa. Rhita terbunuh dan dikuburkan prajurit Arthur di bawah tumpukan batu (carnedd) di puncak, yang menurut cerita memberi nama gunung itu: \"gŵydd\" (tumpukan batu) dengan \"ma\" (tempat) menjadi \"Gwyddfa\".",
      "en": "Rhita Gawr is a figure of Welsh folk tales, a giant said to own a cloak made from the beards of kings. He demanded King Arthur's beard; Arthur refused and they fought on Yr Wyddfa. Rhita was killed and Arthur's soldiers buried him under a cairn on the summit, which by the story named the mountain: 'gŵydd', cairn, joined to 'ma', place, giving 'Gwyddfa'.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "rhita-gawr-c01"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Rhita juga dikaitkan dengan Meirionnydd dan didaftar dalam risalah tentang raksasa Wales karya Siôn Dafydd Rhys dari akhir abad ke-16; menurut satu versi ia tinggal di Yr Wyddfa dan dikubur di puncak Aran Benllyn dekat Llyn Tegid. Artikel ja menyamakannya dengan Ritho dalam Historia Regum Britanniae karya Geoffrey dari Monmouth, raksasa yang membuat mantel bulu dari janggut raja-raja yang dibunuhnya dan dikalahkan Arthur.",
      "en": "Rhita is also tied to Meirionnydd and is listed in Siôn Dafydd Rhys's late 16th-century essay on the giants of Wales; one version has him living on Yr Wyddfa and buried on top of Aran Benllyn near Llyn Tegid. The ja article identifies him with Ritho of Geoffrey of Monmouth's Historia Regum Britanniae, a giant who made a fur cloak from the beards of the kings he slew and was overcome by Arthur.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "rhita-gawr-c02",
        "rhita-gawr-c03",
        "rhita-gawr-c04"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,
  "etymology": null,
  "story_mode": {

  },

    "id": "Menurut cerita rakyat, Yr Wyddfa (Snowdon) dinamai menurut tumpukan batu di atas makam Rhita Gawr.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "rhita-gawr-c01",
      "source_id": "rhita-gawr-s1",
      "quote": "Cymeriad mewn chwedlau gwerin Cymreig oedd Rhita Gawr. Dywedir fod gan y cawr hwn fantell wedi ei gwneud o farfau brenhinoedd. Hawliodd farf y Brenin Arthur. Gwrthododd Arthur, a bu ymladdfa rhyngddynt ar yr Wyddfa.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Rhita Gawr, raksasa (cawr) cerita rakyat Welsh (Cymreig, Wales); mantel janggut raja; menuntut janggut Raja (Brenin) Arthur; bertarung di Yr Wyddfa (Snowdon); dimakamkan di bawah carnedd; gŵydd + ma = Gwyddfa.",
        "en": "Rhita Gawr, a giant of Welsh folk tales (Wales); cloak of kings' beards; demanded King Arthur's beard; fought on Yr Wyddfa (Snowdon); buried under a carnedd; gŵydd + ma = Gwyddfa."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rhita-gawr-c02",
      "source_id": "rhita-gawr-s1",
      "quote": "Mae'n cael ei restru mewn traethawd ar gewri Cymru gan Siôn Dafydd Rhys a ysgrifennodd ar ddiwedd yr 16g.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Didaftar Siôn Dafydd Rhys akhir abad ke-16; Meirionnydd; dimakamkan di Aran Benllyn dekat Llyn Tegid.",
        "en": "Listed by Siôn Dafydd Rhys, late 16th century; Meirionnydd; buried on Aran Benllyn near Llyn Tegid."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rhita-gawr-c03",
      "source_id": "rhita-gawr-s2",
      "quote": "Rhita Gawr (pe Rhudda Gawr, ar ramz ruz) zo un haroz eus mojennoù Kembre. Graet e vantell gant barvioù rouaned.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel br: Rhita Gawr (Rhudda Gawr), tokoh legenda Wales; mantel dari janggut raja.",
        "en": "br article: Rhita Gawr (Rhudda Gawr), a figure of Welsh legend; cloak of kings' beards."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "rhita-gawr-c04",
      "source_id": "rhita-gawr-s3",
      "quote": "ジェフリー・オブ・モンマスの『ブリタニア列王史』によると、リトーは自分が殺した王たちのひげで毛皮の外套をつくり、アーサー王にすみやかに自分のひげをはぎ取って自分に差し出すように命じた。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel ja: Ritho dalam Historia Regum Britanniae karya Geoffrey dari (of) Monmouth; mantel dari janggut raja; dikalahkan Arthur.",
        "en": "ja article: Ritho in Geoffrey of Monmouth's Historia Regum Britanniae; cloak of kings' beards; defeated by Arthur."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Kurcaci Simonside, juga disebut Brownmen, Bogles dan Duergar, adalah ras kurcaci dalam folklor Inggris dari Simonside Hills, Northumberland.",
    "en": "The Simonside Dwarfs, also called Brownmen, Bogles and Duergar, are a race of dwarfs in English folklore from the Simonside Hills of Northumberland.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "simonside-dwarfs-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Kurcaci Simonside, juga dikenal sebagai Brownmen, Bogles dan Duergar, adalah ras kurcaci dalam folklor Inggris yang terutama dikaitkan dengan Simonside Hills di Northumberland, Inggris utara; pemimpin mereka konon bernama Heslop. Dalam kisah tradisional The Duergar yang dituturkan Frederick Grice (1944), salah satunya digambarkan pendek, bermantel kulit domba, bercelana kulit tikus mondok, dan bertopi lumut yang dihiasi bulu.",
      "en": "The Simonside Dwarfs, also known as Brownmen, Bogles and Duergar, are a race of dwarfs in English folklore tied especially to Northumberland's Simonside Hills in the north of England, and said to be led by one Heslop. In Frederick Grice's 1944 retelling of the traditional tale The Duergar, one is short, wearing a lambskin coat, moleskin trousers and a moss hat stuck with a feather.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "simonside-dwarfs-c01",
        "simonside-dwarfs-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Mereka senang menyesatkan para pengembara, terutama setelah gelap, sering membawa obor menyala untuk menuntun mereka ke rawa, mirip Will-o'-the-wisp, dan menghilang saat fajar. Kata duergar kemungkinan berasal dari kata dialek perbatasan Inggris-Skotlandia untuk \"kurcaci\" ditambah akhiran jamak Norse -ar, atau dari kata Norse Kuno dvergar. Artikel it menyebut mereka ras kurcaci yang jelek dan jahat.",
      "en": "They loved to lead travellers astray, above all after dark, often carrying lit torches to draw them into bogs like a Will-o'-the-wisp, and would vanish at dawn. The word duergar probably comes from Border dialect words for 'dwarf' with a mistakenly added Norse plural -ar, or from Old Norse dvergar. The it article calls them an ugly, particularly wicked race of dwarfs.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "simonside-dwarfs-c03",
        "simonside-dwarfs-c04",
        "simonside-dwarfs-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Skuld adalah putri dalam legenda Skandinavia, saudari tiri Raja Denmark Hrólfr Kraki dan istri pembunuhnya, Hjörvarðr; dalam saga ia penyihir.",
    "en": "Skuld is a princess of Scandinavian legend, half-sister of the Danish king Hrólfr Kraki and wife of his killer Hjörvarðr; the sagas make her a sorceress.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "skuld-princess-c01",
      "skuld-princess-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Skuld (juga Sculd, Skulde atau Skulda) adalah putri dalam legenda Skandinavia, saudari atau saudari tiri Raja Denmark legendaris Hrólfr Kraki dan istri pembunuh Hrólfr, Hjörvarðr. Dalam beberapa sumber, seperti Hrólfs saga kraka, ia menghasut suaminya untuk menggulingkan raja. Namanya, sama dengan Skuld salah satu norn, berasal dari kata kerja Norse Kuno skulla, \"harus/akan\". Menurut saga itu, ia putri Helgi dengan perempuan elf.",
      "en": "Skuld (also Sculd, Skulde or Skulda) is a princess in Scandinavian legend; she was a full or half sister of Hrólfr Kraki, the legendary Danish king, and married Hjörvarðr, the man who killed him. Several sources, among them Hrólfs saga kraka, have her urge her husband to topple the king. Her name, shared with Skuld the norn, comes from the Old Norse verb skulla, 'shall, ought to be'. In that saga she is Helgi's daughter by an elf-woman.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "skuld-princess-c01",
        "skuld-princess-c02",
        "skuld-princess-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Skuld mengumpulkan pasukan besar berisi prajurit kuat, penjahat, elf dan norn, dan memakai seiðr (sihir) untuk menyembunyikannya dari Hrólfr. Mereka menyerang Lejre saat Yule; Skuld merapal mantra terkuat untuk membangkitkan prajuritnya yang gugur, hingga Hrólfr dan sebelas jagoannya tewas. Ia menjadi Ratu Denmark tetapi memerintah buruk dan singkat, sebelum ditangkap pasukan Swedia dan disiksa hingga mati sebagai balasan.",
      "en": "Skuld raised a great army of strong warriors, outlaws, elves and norns and used seiðr, sorcery, to hide it from Hrólfr. They fell on Lejre at Yule; Skuld cast her mightiest spell to raise her fallen warriors, until Hrólfr and eleven of his champions died. She became Queen of Denmark but ruled badly and briefly before a Swedish force seized her and she was tortured to death in revenge.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "skuld-princess-c03",
        "skuld-princess-c04",
        "skuld-princess-c06",
        "skuld-princess-c07"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "skuld-princess-c01",
      "source_id": "skuld-princess-s1",
      "quote": "was a princess of Scandinavian legend, the sister or half-sister of the legendary Danish King Hrólfr Kraki and the wife of Hrólfr's killer, Hjörvarðr",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Skuld (Sculd, Skulde, Skulda), putri legenda Skandinavia (Scandinavian), saudari tiri Raja Denmark (Danish, Denmark) Hrólfr Kraki, istri Hjörvarðr.",
        "en": "Skuld (Sculd, Skulde, Skulda), princess of Scandinavian legend, half-sister of the Danish King Hrólfr Kraki, wife of Hjörvarðr."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "skuld-princess-c02",
      "source_id": "skuld-princess-s1",
      "quote": "The name Skuld (which the princess shares with Skuld, one of the norns in Norse mythology) is derived from the Old Norse verb skulla, \"need/ought to be/shall be\"",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Skuld, sama dengan norn, dari Norse Kuno (Old Norse) skulla; Hrólfs saga kraka.",
        "en": "The name Skuld, shared with the norn, from Old Norse skulla; Hrólfs saga kraka."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "skuld-princess-c03",
      "source_id": "skuld-princess-s1",
      "quote": "She was a great sorceress and used seiðr (witchcraft) to hide the great muster from Hrólfr and his champions.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penyihir besar, memakai seiðr; pasukan prajurit, penjahat, elf dan norn.",
        "en": "A great sorceress using seiðr; an army of warriors, criminals, elves and norns."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "skuld-princess-c04",
      "source_id": "skuld-princess-s1",
      "quote": "As Hjörvarðr was also killed in the battle, Skuld became the Queen of Denmark but did not rule well and only for a short time.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hjörvarðr juga tewas; Skuld menjadi Ratu Denmark (Queen of Denmark) tetapi memerintah buruk dan singkat.",
        "en": "Hjörvarðr was also killed; Skuld became Queen of Denmark but ruled badly and briefly."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "skuld-princess-c05",
      "source_id": "skuld-princess-s2",
      "quote": "Egli la violentò e la mise incinta di una figlia che fu chiamata Skuld.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel it: Skuld putri Helgi dengan perempuan elf.",
        "en": "it article: Skuld daughter of Helgi by an elf-woman."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "skuld-princess-c06",
      "source_id": "skuld-princess-s1",
      "quote": "To overcome him, Skuld cast the most powerful spell to resuscitate her fallen warriors and after a long fight Hrólfr and eleven of his champions fell",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Lejre saat Yule, Skuld merapal mantra terkuat membangkitkan prajurit yang gugur; Hrólfr dan sebelas jagoan tewas.",
        "en": "At Lejre at Yule, Skuld cast her strongest spell to revive fallen warriors; Hrólfr and eleven champions fell."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "skuld-princess-c07",
      "source_id": "skuld-princess-s1",
      "quote": "a vengeance was exacted on Skuld in retribution for her treachery and fratricide, as she was tortured to death",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ditangkap pasukan Swedia (Swedish), Skuld disiksa hingga mati sebagai balasan.",
        "en": "Seized by a Swedish army, Skuld was tortured to death in retribution."
      }
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Wirry-cow adalah istilah Skotlandia untuk bugbear, goblin, hantu, ghoul atau sosok menakutkan lainnya, kadang juga Iblis atau orang-orangan sawah.",
    "en": "Wirry-cow is a Scottish word for a bugbear, goblin, ghost, ghoul or other frightful thing, sometimes also the Devil or a scarecrow.",

    ]

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Di Skotlandia, wirry-cow adalah bugbear, goblin, hantu, ghoul atau sosok menakutkan lainnya; kadang istilah itu dipakai untuk Iblis atau orang-orangan sawah. Allan Ramsay memakainya dalam bait \"They looked like wirry-cows\", dan Sir Walter Scott memakainya dalam novel Guy Mannering.",
      "en": "In Scotland a wirry-cow is a bugbear, goblin, ghost, ghoul or any other frightening thing, and the word is at times applied to the Devil or a scarecrow. Allan Ramsay used it in the line 'They looked like wirry-cows', and Sir Walter Scott in his novel Guy Mannering.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wirry-cow-c01",
        "wirry-cow-c02",
        "wirry-cow-c03"

    },
    {
      "id": "John Jamieson menurunkan kata ini dari worry (Skots modern wirry) dalam arti lama \"mengganggu\", dari bahasa Inggris Kuno wyrgan, ditambah cowe, \"hobgoblin, sesuatu yang menakutkan\". Artikel it mencatat bentuk lain wirry-carl.",
      "en": "John Jamieson derives the word from worry (modern Scots wirry) in its old sense of harassing, from Old English wyrgan, plus cowe, 'a hobgoblin, an object of terror'. The it article records the variant wirry-carl.",
